Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The matter operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 495. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 13 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to matter structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 495/13 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
